# Blueprint traceability matrix

Generated by `qip blueprint render` from the requirement catalogue and
`docs/blueprint/assessment/*.json`. **Do not edit by hand.** This is the live
register (ADR 0099), and there is no second one.

**1518 requirements; 1518 applicable** (OBSOLETE excluded from the denominator).

| Integrated (reached from a composition root) | 659 | 43.4% |
| Deployable (provisionable from committed config) | 184 | 12.1% |
| End-to-end demonstrated | 0 | 0.0% |

Egress: trust-zones allows ibm-quantum only from the optimisation zone. Code side: HostedProvider has no transport and reports unavailable; no asynchronous gateway/queue exists; no edge or money crate can reach a solver. | PARTIAL | Gateway service with queue, credential and transport. |  | P1 | cargo test -p qip-acceptance --test architecture --test stress |  |
